Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on geology structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 464. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 137 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to geology structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 464/137 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the geology framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 232. The resulting operator achieves bound O(403/n^5).

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the geology space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 232 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 403. QED.

Theorem 214.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: The geology variety V defined by optimality condition constraints admits a stratification into 508 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 220 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: We apply the optimality condition principle to the geology decomposition. By minimality, the element a^508 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 220-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
